- **error-0308010c-digital-envelope-routin-39ffaead4e2f62e2**: Fixes error:0308010C digital envelope routines unsupported after a Node upgrade broke a webpack 4 build. Use it when a previously green frontend build starts failing at hash time right after moving to Node 17 or newer. Key trigger: webpack 4 hashes with MD4, which OpenSSL 3 removed from its default providers, so the fix is the legacy provider flag or an upgrade to webpack 5. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/error-0308010c-digital-envelope-routin-39ffaead4e2f62e2))
- **error-0308010c-digital-envelope-routin-b6342368710d2dc9**: A step-by-step skill for fixing Node's 'error:0308010C:digital envelope routines::unsupported' build failure: the OpenSSL 3 vs webpack 4 md4 clash. Covers the NODE_OPTIONS legacy-provider unblock and the real fix of upgrading the toolchain. Use when builds break after moving to Node 17+. Triggers: 'digital envelope routines', 'error:0308010C', 'md4', 'createHash'. Not for: runtime TLS errors, expired certificates, or non-Node build failures. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/error-0308010c-digital-envelope-routin-b6342368710d2dc9))
- **error-0a000086-ssl-routines-certificat-4242ea5ccab3de37**: A fix for OpenSSL's generic 'certificate verify failed' (error:0A000086): how to extract the specific verify code with -verify_return_error, what the common codes mean (missing intermediate vs stale CA bundle), and how to fix each side. Not for: expired certs specifically, TLS version errors, or cipher mismatches. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/error-0a000086-ssl-routines-certificat-4242ea5ccab3de37))

- **error-26078-state-15**: In XDBHost, when a login request is dequeued we try to ensure that the login has not been in the queue for more than 10 seconds. If the login has remained in the queue for 10 seconds, xdbhost forcibly closes the named pipe connection with SQL and clears the socket duplication queue. While this is in progress, all logins in the queue fail with 26078/15 and all new incoming logins are failed with 40613/13. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/connectivity/connectivity-errors/error-26078-state-15) · ★1)

- **error-40613-state-10**: This error occurs when one or more gateway instances is redirecting login connections to a DB node where the SQL instance is unavailable or does not exist. The error means the XDBHost process on the node is unable to find the SQL instance requested by the client. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/connectivity/connectivity-errors/error-40613-state-10) · ★1)
- **error-40613-state-13**: This error indicates a failure to enqueue logins within XDBHost. XDBHost maintains a queue per sql instance. This queue holds the login requests that are destined to the particular SQL instance. In order to operate on the queue, threads running in XDBHost attempt to acquire a lock before enqueue/dequeue operations. The lock has a timeout of 5 seconds. A failure to acquire the lock results in the login being failed. This particular condition is logged by xdbhost as 40613/13. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/connectivity/connectivity-errors/error-40613-state-13) · ★1)
- **error-40613-state-15**: In XDBHost, when a login request is dequeued we try to ensure that the login has not been in the queue for more than 10 seconds. If the login has remained in the queue for 10 seconds, xdbhost forcibly closes the named pipe connection with SQL and clears the socket duplication queue. While this is in progress, all logins in the queue fail with 40613/15 and all new incoming logins are failed with 40613/13. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/connectivity/connectivity-errors/error-40613-state-15) · ★1)

- **error-40613-state-81**: Diagnoses login error 40613 with state 81 on PaaSv2 clusters caused by IPv6 DIP prefix mismatch. The error occurs when the PaaSv2VnetIPv6Prefix configured in XTS does not match the actual IPv6 prefix on VM NICs, causing VNet Service Endpoint packets to be incorrectly classified during login. Mitigation involves running a CAB to override the config prefix. This is a platform-side infrastructure issue, not a customer configuration problem. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/connectivity/connectivity-errors/error-40613-state-81) · ★1)
